```text
WAAD-BECK systems LTD
GENERAL
M529 COMMUNICATIONS_MODULE
 .
.. .
The M529 Communications Module provides the required talkback, cue feed and test oscillator facilities for the ST series of Stereo Television consoles
The integral three watt cue amplifier receives inputs from the console prefade Listen bus and external talkback facilities such as an external intercom system Two of the external inputs are selected via solid state switches while a third is a direct input
The cue amplifier output consists of an actively balanced +8 dBm headset feed and an unbalanced 8 ohm speaker connection A mute relay is prov ided for the speaker feed The muting of the speaker may be achieved directly on the module or by external control circuitry .  / (?   . The talkback amplifier amplifies the microphone signal to a standard +8 dBm Line level An integral limiter maintains a relatively constant output signal level over wide ranges of input level vari- ations_ A Normal and Overload LED indicator is provided to show when the input microphone sensiti vity adjustment is correctly set
The talkback amplifier provides a direct output, assignable slate and select functions , 4. ' (i A slate feed is assignable through master channel preselectors_ Talk- back to the console auxiliary outputs, mon itors two, three and four, as well as a spare output are available through individual mom- entry pushbutton selectors.
A ten selection multifrequency oscillator is part of the M529 module. The oscillator connects to the master slating control circuitry and to switchable direct output feeds _ The oscillator output selector has a red LED indicator which illuminates when a tone signal is leaving the module These outputs are normally wired to the ST console integral jackfield  .  ' ^ . s  , `  The M529 is packaged in the standard WBS module size of 1.60" wide X 14.15" high X 6.50" deep _
The power for the module is a unipolar +25 volts which is taken from one side of the console bipolar power supplies_    P7 i . . 
<.:
2-1
```

```text
CIRcUIT DESCRIPT ION
M529 COMMUNICATIONS MODULE
Speaker Amplifier Inputs
The actively balanced preamplifier u3 has four inputs which may be connected to it. Two switched inputs appearing at P2 pins 4 and D and 5 and E These inputs are controlled by solid state switches u2 The crosspoints are normally off and may be selected by grounding control inputs P2 pins 7 and H_ A direct input which is not switchable appears at P2 pins 3 and C_ These three inputs have a nominal input signal level of +8 dBm and provide a bridging load to the source A summing input is provided as well at P2 pins 1 and A This input is designed to accept the prefade Listen bus feeds from the M520 and M521 Input Modules _ 3 ` The preamplifier output is coupled through C14 to the interstate level control R32
Speaker Amplifier Output
: .
Integrated circuit u4 is the output amplifier. This device is capable of developing a maximum output signal level of three watts into an 8 ohm load_
The input to this amplifier interstate level control R32 _
is
coupled through C15 from the . . " |
An acti vely balanced headset feed is coupled through C24 and C25 9 appearing at P2 pins 10 and L The nominal output level is +8 dBm.  . The 8 o unbalanced ohm speaker feed is coupled through C23 and the normally closed contacts of mute relay K1 This output appears at P2 pin 12  .  . `" '^ 6 '~ Relay Control  '  . Mute  R0 F . ^ . . The mute relay K1 is driven 01 .' by transistors and 02 The LED DSI Mllum ina tes anytime the relay is operated_
Driver transistor 02 receives its control input directly from the integ- ral mute switch SW16 2 03  or through  .  :  ". '. .  . . The external mute control input appearing at P2 pin 8 will turn on relay K1 when grounded to control common This input will still mute the relay even through three diode drops (approximately 2 volts) The external mute control input may be inhibited by connecting P2 pin J to control common It should be noted that the operation of the integral mute switch SW16 overrides the external control circuitry.
```

```text
CIRcUIT DESCRIPTION

M529 COMMUNICATIONS MODULE
Talkback Preampl ifier
4;.
P~vy Vo
..
Talkback Selectors
The integral gooseneck microphone connects to the input transformer T1 of the preamplifier circuitry_ The secondary of T1 is coupled through C28 to the preamplifier integrated circuit USA . The gain of this preamplifier circuit may be varied over a 30 dB range via the setting of R67 . This trim control is accessible from the front panel of the module .` ;
The talkback amplifier output is also fed to selectors SW8 through SW15_ These pushbuttons allow the operator to talk to the four auxiliary outputs, monitors two, three and four and to a spare output.
A diode isolated control output is provided to operate the external talkback relays or feed information into the ST series console control system.
The preamplifier output is coupled through C35 to the limiter circuit.  . .. Talkback Limiter
Slate The output from the talkback amplifier and the SA14-124 Oscillator Subassembly are fed to the Slate and Tone selectors SW2 and Sw3 _
FET 04 , is used as variable resistor in the limiter circui try, which controls or maintains a constant output Tevel from the preamplifier over a wide range of input level variations
The talk control will override the tone selection Selectors SW4 through to SW7 are assign preset selectors which determine to which master outputs the slating is fed to. These selectors have diode isolated control outputs for the purpose of opera ting the external slating relays. (These relays are part of the M527 Master Module)  Oscillator
USB is a buffering amplifier which isolates 04 from the output circuitry. u6 amplifies the audio output signal which is fed back to rectifiers CR6 and CR7 The error voltage developed across C37 varies the resistance of 04 and therefore the signal level fed to the input of USB Threshold control R84 sets the level at which the limiting begins
The correction voltage across C37 is fed to amplifier U7 through R83 The output of u7 drives the 1 imiter level indicator comparator U8A and U8B R88 sets the threshold point at which the comparators will switch. The green LED of DS2 is set to illuminate at the threshold of Limi ting. ' ~ .' ` -. '
The 5A14-124 Oscillator Subassembly feeds the slating functions and the external assignable output. '
Selector Sw1 assigns the oscillator output to the external feeds through the appropriate loss networks. Three different output levels are made available from the module +8 dBm 600 ohms, 0 dBm 600 ohms and ~60 dBm 150 ohms_
The red LED of DS2 will illuminate (and the green extinguishes) when the degree of limiting reaches 25 to 30 dB, indicating an over- load condition This will occur if the preamplifier sensitivity control R67 is set too high. ' . < Rn v .. .  . t.  . Talkback Output
Two red LED indicators DS3 and DSL illuminate on the M529 Communi- cations Module anytime the oscillator output is assigned or selected to leave the module.  . ` . .
The output from the imiter is coupled through C5o to the output power amplifier u9 U9B forms the lower half of the actively balanced output amplifier and U9A forms the upper invert ing section The actively balanced output is coupled through C59 and C60 to P1 pins 55 and 56 of the module connector This is the direct output from the talkback amplifier and operates at a nominal level of +8 dBm _ . .  s  ' . 4; . . 4o. . . .
Power Supply Decoupling
The unipolar +25 volt supply is applied to P2 pin 21 This supply voltage is fed through fuse F1 and isolation diode CR37 to several decoupl ing networks.  . .
4-2
4-3
```

### Page 24 (OCR, spell-checked: 2 words corrected)

```text
WAAD-BECK systems LTD:
CIRcUIT DESCRIPT ION
SA14-124 OSC ILLATOR ASSEMBLY
The SA14-124 is a ten frequency oscillator subassembly which plugs onto the M529 Communications Module main circuit board_
U1 with C1 through C4 and the resistors associated with SWI (RI to R22) form a Wien bridge oscillator This resistive capactive network provides positive feedback around U1 at one select frequency _ Q1 , R23 R24 , and R26 provide negative feedback around U1 01 is configured as a variable resistive element _ The negative feedback component around 41 controls the amplitude of the oscillation and therefore its operation in a Linear region where distortion products are very low  ..  u2 amplifies the output of U1 and feeds the audio signal to the full-wave rectifier CRI/CR2 _ The developed error voltage is stored on capacitors C8 and C9, which in turn is applied to Q1's gate, controlling its resistance.   `
AGC control R24 sets the balance between the oscillator positive and negative feedback. With the AGC function disabled R24 is adjusted so that the oscillator output level is just at the point of clipping AGC level control R4O is then set to optimize the output level versus frequency selection R36 is adjusted with the oscillator selected to 30 Hz for symmetrical full-wave rectification at CRI/CR2 cathodes.
The oscillator output is coupled through C14 to the output level trim control R42_ R42 is accessible through the front panel of the M529 module for fine adjustment of the output level 
u3 and u4 form an actively balanced output amplifier. The output is coupled through C22 and C23 to assembly connector P1 pins 5 and 6 _  The supply voltage appearing at P1 pin 1 of the assembly connector is decoupled by the network R54 , C24 and C25_ 

 .
 7^



 ...
''
.
. ,

.
```
